# Armor
Aliases: Armor Set, Armor Sets, Armor sets, Armors, Armour, Chain Armor, Farming Armor, Farming Armour, Farming armor, Farming armour, Fishing Armor, Fishing Armour, Fishing armor, Foraging Armor, Foraging armor, Gold Armor, Mining Amor, Mining Armor, Mining Armour, Mining amor, Mining armor, Mining armour
Categories: Armor, Armor Sets, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeons, Dungeon Armor, Confirmations needed, Mechanics, Items, Defense, Common items, Uncommon items, Rare items, Epic items, Legendary items, Health
[Armor]
Category: [[Armor & Equipment]]
Armor is a category of equippable items that increase a [[Player|player's]] [[Stats]] when put into the appropriate Armor slot. Armor pieces comes in 4 categories: [[Helmets]], [[Chestplates]], [[Leggings]] and [[Boots]].
## Overview
- In SkyBlock, "Armor" is an imprecise term that refers to either armor piece(s) or armor set(s) or the concept of Armor.
- An "Armor Piece refers to either a [[Helmets|Helmet]], a [[Chestplates|Chestplate]], a pair of [[Leggings]] or a pair of [[Boots]].
- An "Armor Set" refers to a combination of armor pieces, often sharing (similar) [[Stats]], source, purpose, Set Bonus. This may also include [[Equipment]] pieces.
### Bonus
- A "Bonus" is a buff that is granted if the player wears armor pieces bearing that same Bonus.
  - A "Piece Bonus" is a buff that is granted if the player wears the armor piece.
    - An "Ability" is a synonym for "Piece Bonus".
  - A "Full Set" Bonus will only be granted if the player wears ALL of the armor pieces belonging to that armor set.
  - A "Tiered" Bonus will be granted if the player wears part(s) of the armor pieces belonging to that armor set.
    - The Bonus may have a minimum amount of armor pieces to be active.
    - The Bonus typically becomes more powerful as the player wears more armor pieces of that armor set.
- If the conditions are NOT fulfilled for the Bonus to be active, then the Bonus name in the item lore will be greyed out.
- If the conditions are fulfilled for the Bonus to be active, then the Bonus name in the item lore will be golden.
Examples:
Piece Bonus: Lapis Boots ([[Lapis Armor]])' Piece Bonus: Magnetic; → grants +50% [[Experience]] from Mining Ores.
Piece Bonus: Ender Chestplate ([[Ender Armor]]); → grants 2x its stat while on [[The End]], though it lacks the mention of Piece Bonus. For consistency's sake, it is included here.
Ability: Tater Boots ([[Tater Armor]])'s Ability: Farmer's Grace; → prevents the player from trampling crops.
Full Set Bonus: Glacite Helmet ([[Glacite Armor]]), Glacite Chestplate ([[Glacite Armor]]), Glacite Leggings ([[Glacite Armor]]) and Glacite Boots ([[Glacite Armor]]) all share the Full Set Bonus: Expert Miner; → only active when the player wears all 4 of these pieces, granting +2 [[Mining Speed]] per [[Mining]] level.
Tiered Bonus: Backwater Helmet ([[Backwater Armor]]), Backwater Chestplate ([[Backwater Armor]]), Backwater Leggings ([[Backwater Armor]]) and Backwater Boots ([[Backwater Armor]]) all share the Tiered Bonus: Swamp Soldier; → +5% Damage against Sea Creatures at 1 armor piece worn.; → +10% Damage against Sea Creatures at 2 armor pieces worn.; → +15% Damage against Sea Creatures at 3 armor pieces worn.; → +20% Damage against Sea Creatures at 4 armor pieces worn.; → […]; → +40% Damage against Sea Creatures at 4 armor pieces worn and 4 equipment pieces of the same set.
Tiered Bonus: Bronze Hunter Helmet ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]), Bronze Hunter Chestplate ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]), Bronze Hunter Leggings ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]) and Bronze Hunter Boots ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]) all share the Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty.; → only active if the player wears 2 or more of the [[Bronze Hunter Armor]], preventing the player from fishing up, damaging or being damaged by Sea Creatures.
Inactive Bonus: Wearing 1 piece of the [[Bronze Hunter Armor]]; → Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty (1/2).
Active Bonus: Wearing 3 pieces of the [[Bronze Hunter Armor]]; → Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty (3/2).
- Some Armor Pieces do not belong to any Armor Set.
- Some Armors do not have any Bonus.
### Controls
- An Armor Piece may be equipped:
  - by Right-Clicking while holding it.
    - This will automatically place it in its appropriate slot.
    - This does not work if the appropriate slot is already occupied.
  - Left-Clicking, Right-Clicking or Drop Key it in the inventory while the /stats menu opened.
    - This will automatically place it in its appropriate slot.
    - If the appropriate slot is already busy, this will replace it and the previous piece of equipment will be returned in the inventory.
- An Armor Piece may be un-equipped:
  - by clicking it in the inventory;
  - by clicking it in the /stats menu.
- The [[Wardrobe]] can be used to store and freely swap between Armor Sets in a click.
- The [[Loadouts]] can be used to freely swap between Armor Sets, Equipment Sets, Power Stones, Tuning Points in a click.
## Upgrades
These upgrades can be applied to any armor piece, increasing its [[Stats]] and boosts.
|Example|Image|Upgrade|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|Smart Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Anvil]]|Reforges ([[Reforging]])|Reforges ([[Reforging]]) range in effects, and give stats, and sometimes an extra ability. The power of the reforge depending on the [[Rarity]] of the item. Reforges cost more [[Coins]] to apply at higher rarities.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Enchanting]]|[[Enchantments]]|[[Enchantments]] grant a variety of effects and stat boosts, and some give extra abilities. They can be applied to Armor in an Anvil, and cost [[Experience]] to combine.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Enchanting]]|[[Ultimate Enchantments]]|[[Ultimate Enchantments]] are better than ordinary enchantments, and give a unique, and usually powerful, bonus. However, only one Ultimate Enchantment can be applied per piece.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Hot Potato Book]]|[[Hot Potato Book]]|Hot Potato Books ([[Hot Potato Book]]) grant +4 [[Health]] and +2 [[Defense]] each. They can be applied up to 10 times per piece.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Fuming Potato Book]]|[[Fuming Potato Book]]|Fuming Potato Books ([[Fuming Potato Book]]) function the same way as Hot Potato Books, as they grant +4 [[Health]] and +2 [[Defense]] each. Can be applied up to 5 times on a piece of armor, on top of the bonus from Hot Potato Books.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[The Art of Peace]]|[[The Art of Peace]]|[[The Art of Peace]] can be applied once to an armor piece to grant +40 [[Health]].|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]s upgrade the rarity of an armor piece by 1, thus amplifying its reforge power. They can only be used once per piece.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Gemstone]]|[[Gemstone]]|Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) add stats to items. They can only be applied to pieces with a corresponding [[Gemstone Slot]]. Their power ranges depending on the tier of Gemstone.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate ✪✪✪✪✪|[[Wither Essence]]|[[Essence]]|[[Essence]] can be used to dungeonize and star Armor. This grants an extra stat boost inside and out of a Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]).|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate ✪✪✪✪✪➊|[[Master Star]]|[[Master Star]]|Master Stars ([[Master Star]]) can be used to further dungeonize an armor piece. This goes up to an additional 5 stars, and gives a larger stat boost when inside Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) Dungeons.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Bonzo Fragment]]/[[Scarf Fragment]]/[[Livid Fragment]]|Fragments|Certain dungeon armor pieces can be fragged, which means they can be upgraded into a higher tier version of the same item, but with better stats.|
|Shadow Assassin Chestplate|[[Runes]]|[[Runes]]|[[Runes]] are purely cosmetic items that can be applied to certain armor pieces to give them particle effects.|

### Vanilla Armor
This is the original Minecraft armor. Defense point values correspond to the number of armor points given in vanilla gameplay. These armor sets are recommended to be never used, as there are much better alternatives.

## Trivia
- The Full Set Bonus of certain Armor Sets can be turned off by middle clicking an armor piece in your inventory while wearing it. This will last until the player swaps or un-equips at least a single armor piece.

# Chestplates

Categories: Armor, Armor Sets, Chestplate
[Chestplates]
Type: [[Armor]]
The Chestplate Slot is the most powerful of the four [[Armor]] slots. There are very few standalone pieces for this slot.

# Dragon Armor
Aliases: Dragon Armors
Categories: Armor, The End, Ender
Dragon Armor is a set of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] sets that are obtained from defeating their respective [[Ender Dragon]] or by crafting them with their respective [[Dragon Fragment|Dragon Fragments]]. Each set of Dragon Armor has a full set bonus that corresponds to its purpose.
All sets of Dragon Armor, with the exception of [[Superior Dragon Armor]], require [[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] to use. [[Superior Dragon Armor]] requires [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] instead.

## Trivia
- The total cost of all the sets is from fragments from the bazaar. Most of the total coins come from the cost of the raw materials for [[Superior Dragon Armor]].
- [[Holy Dragon Armor]] is the only set of Dragon Armor which does not drop from an [[Ender Dragon]], as there are no Holy Dragons. Instead, they are dropped from [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]] wearing Holy Dragon Armor in the [[Catacombs]].

# Hatcessories
Aliases: Hatccessory, Hatcessory
Categories: Armor, Accessories, Helmet
A Hatcessories are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items that functions as both a [[Helmets|Helmet]] and an [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It can be either worn on the player's head or placed in the [[Accessory Bag]]. They grant +1 [[Intelligence]] for every SkyBlock year the player's profile has existed, but only when worn as Helmet.
The Hatcessories do not stack with each other and only one will provide stat boost and [[Accessory Power]].
## Obtaining
Each [[SkyBlock Anniversary]], [[Simon]] shows up in [[Hub Island|The Hub]] and gives a unique Hatcessory with a random color or expression.
## List of Hatcessories
|Image|Name|Year|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Crab Hat of Celebration]]|[[Crab Hat of Celebration]]|2020||
|[[Crab Hat of Celebration]]|Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition ([[Crab Hat of Celebration]])|2022|Animated when worn|
|[[Sloth Hat of Celebration]]|[[Sloth Hat of Celebration]]|2023||
|[[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]|[[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]|2024|Holds a balloon of the same color when worn|
|[[6th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]|[[6th Anniversary Balloon Hat]]|2025||
|[[7th Anniversary Cake Hat]]|[[7th Anniversary Cake Hat]]|2026||
## Trivia
- The name Hatcessory is a partial portmanteau of hat and accessory.
- Hatcessories are inconsistent with their names. The 2022 Crab Hats are the only Hatcessories specifying their year of origin in their title. Crab Hats and Sloth Hats are said to be "of Celebration", while the Balloon Hats are "Anniversary".
- All hatcessories, except Balloon Hats, create particles around the player when worn on their head. Occasionally they also launch a firework as well.
- Sloth Hats come in 10 variants, while every other Hatcessory only has 9.
- If a player wears a Hatcessory on their head while keeping another different, or another of the same, in their accessory bag, they will get [[Magic Find]] from them both. The stats from [[Enrichments]] are also doubled this way.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 10, 2020|Added the [[Crab Hat of Celebration]].|
|Jun 10, 2022|Added the 2022 editions of the [[Crab Hat of Celebration]].|
|Jun 8, 2023|Added the [[Sloth Hat of Celebration]].|
|Jun 10, 2024|Added the [[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]].|

Tooltip: Defense: +75; Ability: Bone Shield A Bone Shield will surround you, nullifying damage you take but consuming a bone in the process.; Bones regenerate every 30 seconds.; This item can be reforged!; RARE HELMET
Monster Raider Armor set is composed of [[Skeleton's Helmet]], [[Guardian Chestplate]], [[Creeper Pants]], and [[Tarantula Boots]]. This set is the upgraded form of the [[Monster Hunter Armor]].
This is one of the "secret" sets, as none of the individual items show that they are part of it beforehand. When the set is complete, each item still has its ability.
This set compared to [[Monster Hunter Armor]] reduces 10% more (25% -> 35%) [[Damage]] from mobs and does 10% more (25% -> 35%) [[Damage]] damage to mobs.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

Spirit Boots are [[Rarity|EPIC]] Dungeon [[Boots]].
## Obtaining
### Thorn Collection
Spirit Boots can be claimed once as a reward upon reaching [[Thorn|Thorn V (5)]], after defeating Thorn 400 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Spirit Boots can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Emerald|3,500,000 [[Coins]]|0.9009% (1 in 111 runs)|0.9091% (1 in 110 runs)|260|1|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Obsidian|4,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.2195% (1 in 82 runs)|2.2066% (1 in 45.3 runs)|260|1|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|3,500,000 [[Coins]]|0.9091% (1 in 110 runs)|1.0343% (1 in 96.7 runs)|260|1|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|4,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.3841% (1 in 72.3 runs)|2.4506% (1 in 40.8 runs)|260|4|
## Usage
Boots:
Spirit Boots [[Health]]: +150 [[Defense]]: +50 [[Strength]]: +25 [[Intelligence]]: +50 [[Speed]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Spirit Glide; Press and hold Sneak to fly around as a spirit.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 250; Cooldown: 60s]
Boots:
[[Spirit Boots]] [[Health]]: +150 [[Defense]]: +50 [[Strength]]: +35 [[Intelligence]]: +50 [[Speed]]: +35
[Effects]
[Ability: Spirit Glide; Press and hold Sneak to fly around as a spirit.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 250; Cooldown: 60s]
The Spirit Boots' ability, Spirit Glide, allows the user to become a spirit and fly for a duration of approximately 5s, granting invincibility during this duration. It costs 250 [[Mana]] and has a 60s cooldown.
## Upgrading
Spirit Boots can be upgraded into Sprit Boots using 8 [[Thorn Fragment|Thorn Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Strength]]
- +10 [[Speed]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

## Tips
- Spirit Boots are useful for granting increased [[Speed]] until one obtains [[Maxor's Armor|Maxor's Boots]]: although [[Maxor's Armor|Maxor's Boots]] grants lower [[Speed]] than Spirit Boots, it also grants higher [[Effective Health]] and [[Crit Damage]] and the Spirit Boots can be substituted for with a [[Black Cat Pet]] or a [[Spooky Cupcake]].

The Spirit Mask is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Dungeon [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Spirit Mask can be found in [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Master Mode - Floor IV]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|15,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.346% (1 in 289 runs)|0.6124% (1 in 163.3 runs)|270|1|
## Usage
Helmet:
Spirit Mask [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +50 [[Intelligence]]: +20 [[Speed]]: +15
[Effects]
[Ability: Second Wind; Instead of dying, gain +50 Speed and damage immunity for 3 seconds.; Cooldown: 30s]
Helmet:
[[Spirit Mask]] [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +75 [[Intelligence]]: +25 [[Speed]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Second Wind; Instead of dying, gain +50 Speed and damage immunity for 3 seconds. Also heals you for 10% of your Health over 5 seconds.; Cooldown: 30s]
When the user takes fatal damage while wearing the Spirit Mask, they will be protected from that damage and all other damage for 3 seconds. In addition, they will gain +50 [[Speed]] for 3 seconds. The ability has a cooldown of 30s.
## Upgrading
A Spirit Mask can be upgraded into Spirit Mask using 8 [[Thorn Fragment|Thorn Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +25 [[Defense]]
- +5 [[Intelligence]]
- +15 [[Speed]]
- Upon proccing Second Wind, heals the wearer for 10% of their [[Health]] over 5s
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Spirit Mask]] 1x Spirit Mask 8x [[Thorn Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Thorn Fragment]] ×8 + Spirit Mask → [[Spirit Mask]]|

|-|-|

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|

|[[Panda Spirit Skin]]|[[Panda Spirit Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - June 15, 2024 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin adapts to the day-night cycle!|
|[[Panda Spirit Skin]]|[[Panda Spirit Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - June 15, 2024 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Wisp Spirit Skin]]|[[Wisp Spirit Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2026 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is swappable between 21 wispy spirited variants!|
|[[Wisp Spirit Skin]]|[[Wisp Spirit Skin]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2026 - 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The texture used for the Spirit Mask resembles Kindred from the game League of Legends.
  - There is also an [[Admin-only]] helmet called the [[Kindred]], which gives +70 [[Speed]].
  - The ability also seems to have a portion of Kindred's ultimate ability, which prevents users from dying. However, unlike the ability in League of Legends, Spirit Mask doesn't heal the player when the effect resolves unless it is upgraded.
- While in the Catacombs, the helmet's cooldown is also reduced by the Mage class' Efficient Spells perk.
- The Spirit Mask pairs well with the [[Bonzo's Mask]], [[Phoenix Pet]], and the [[Mithril Coat]], as the anti-death abilities of all these items can be used with each other.
- The Spirit Mask cannot save players from certain deaths, such as being digested by [[The Matriarch]] or [[Cold|freezing to death]] in [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
- The Spirit Mask is one of two items other than [[Master Star|Master Stars]] and [[Master Skull|Master Skulls]] that are exclusive to Master Mode, the other being the [[Dark Claymore]].
- The Spirit Mask used to be able to be bought at item rounds in a [[Dark Auction]] at a starting price of 20,000,000 [[Coins]].

# Tuxedo
Categories: Armor, 0.7.4
Tuxedos are full outfits (a full set) that can be bought from [[Seymour]] in the [[Village]] underneath [[Taylor]]'s house. They are one of the few sets that don't have a [[Helmets|helmet]]. Any three of the suits are used for the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest as the final task.
Wearing the [[Elegant Tuxedo]] gives the [[Achievements|Achievement]] [[Achievements|Glass Cannon]].
## Suits
|Name|Cost|Rarity|Cr Dmg ([[Crit Damage]])|Int ([[Intelligence]])|Set Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Cheap Tuxedo]]|3M [[Coins]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+100%|+100|Full Set Bonus: Dashing; Max health set to 75 [[Health]] . Deals +50% damage!|
|[[Fancy Tuxedo]]|20M [[Coins]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|+150%|+300|Full Set Bonus: Dashing; Max health set to 150 [[Health]] . Deals +100% damage!|
|[[Elegant Tuxedo]]|75M [[Coins]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|+200%|+500|Full Set Bonus: Dashing; Max health set to 250 [[Health]] . Deals +150% damage!|
## Tips
- If you find yourself constantly dying with this armor set, use a [[Bonzo's Mask]] as a helmet as it gives you double lives in dungeons. However, if you aren't going to do dungeons with this set, you can opt for a [[Spirit Mask]].
- If you want to buy this armor set for the [[Romero and Juliette]] questline, buy the [[Cheap Tuxedo]] as it is the cheapest out of all of them. Hence the name "Cheap" Tuxedo. If that is still too expensive for you, consider buying it from the auction house as it can go for about 1,000,000 [[Coins]] cheaper. Or, if you don't want to do either, you can borrow it from someone.
- The damage from the full set bonuses are part of the [[Damage Calculation|additive multiplier]], hence the actual damage increases are much lower in percentage.
## Trivia
- Each Tuxedo set has a slightly different color, the cheap set has light grey tint, the fancy one, a dark grey tint and the elegant set is black.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 11, 2019|0.7.4|Added Tuxedos.|

# Bestiary
Aliases: Be, Beastiary
Categories: Article stubs, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Mechanics, Combat
[Bestiary]
The Bestiary is a repository for data on [[Mob|Mobs]]. It tracks the player's kills and death against many mobs, rewarding players for reaching certain milestones.
## Interface
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
## Mechanics
The Bestiary tracks the kills of 360 mobs across SkyBlock. Each mob has a family, with some families containing multiple mobs or mob variants.
To start tracking Bestiary kills, it is required to talk to [[Bramass Beastslayer]] on [[Spider's Den]] first.
### Kill Credit
Bestiary credit is awarded when the player assists in killing a mob.
Mobs found in [[Dungeons]] and the [[Kuudra]] boss fight have shared bestiary credit for players in the same instance. The following mobs have shared bestiary credit within the player's dungeon party:
- [[Lost Adventurer]]
- [[Angry Archaeologist]]
- [[Shadow Assassin]]
- [[King Midas]]
- [[Mimic]]
- [[Wither Miner]]
- [[Wither Husk]]
- [[Wither Guard]]
- [[Terracotta]]
- [[Golem|Woke Golem]]
If a player is considered the owner of a mob, such as a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] they fished up, they will always receive credit, whether they dealt damage to the mob or not.
### Brackets
Each family has a certain bracket tier, which determines the number of kills needed to earn Leveling Rewards in the family.
Kill scaling per tier is standardized into brackets, where different tiers require different amounts of kills to earn leveling rewards.
Cumulative Kill Brackets:
|Tier|Brackets 1|Brackets 2|Brackets 3|Brackets 4|Brackets 5|Brackets 6|Brackets 7|Brackets 8|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|20|5|4|2|1|1|1|1|
|2|40|10|8|4|2|2|2|2|
|3|60|15|12|6|3|3|3|3|
|4|100|25|16|10|5|5|5|4|
|5|200|50|20|15|7|7|7|5|
|6|400|100|40|20|10|9|9|8|
|7|800|200|80|25|15|12|11|11|
|8|1,400|350|140|35|20|16|14|14|
|9|2,000|500|200|50|25|20|17|17|
|10|3,000|750|300|75|30|25|20|20|
|11|6,000|1,500|600|150|60|50|30|25|

## Rewards
Two types of rewards can be obtained from the Bestiary.

Leveling rewards are earned after reaching each milestone in a family and provide bonus stats and perks for the family and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].

### Milestone Rewards
Milestone rewards are obtained after reaching 10 family milestones. Each milestone grants +1 [[Health]]. Every 5 milestones grant increasing amounts of [[Combat|Combat XP]]. And every 10 milestones grant [[SkyBlock Levels|+10 SkyBlock XP]]. Rewards are stored until the player claims them, and can accumulate to allow the player to claim multiple milestone rewards at once.
The Combat XP is not affected by [[Combat Wisdom]], it however is affected by all Pet Exp boosts.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

## Trivia
- Due to its absence in internal tracking, the following mobs were not tracked before the Bestiary revamp: [[Kuudra|Kuudra Mobs]], [[Bal]], and [[Reindrake]]. Additionally, the stats of [Lv1] [[Cow]], [[Chicken]], [[Sheep]], [[Pig]], and [[Zombie]] were merged into their minion counterparts and therefore do not count for Bestiary credit.
- If the player has not moved their camera in the past hour, they will not receive Bestiary credit for any mob kills.[Confirm]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 15, 2026|Changed formula for [[Strength]] and [[Magic Find]] from ⌊0.1×(Tier+7.5)2−5.025⌋ to 3×Tier.; So it now gives more [[Strength]] and [[Magic Find]] at lower tiers, equal at Tier XV (15), and less at higher tiers|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Bestiary.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Revamped Bestiary.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Bonus coins per kill for each bestiary mob tier now work correctly.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Bonus XP orbs per kill for each bestiary mob tier have been heavily increased.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|[[Magic Find]] per bestiary mob tier has been rescaled.|
|Aug 15, 2023|Fixed sometimes not being able to gain Combat EXP and Bestiary progress from killing mobs with abilities from weapons such as Hyperion.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Reduced bestiaries for many mobs.|
|Aug 11, 2026|Reduced the kills required for some Hunting Tiers. Hunting 1: 1, 10, 25, 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300 → 1, 5, 10, 20, 35, 50, 65, 85, 105, 125 Hunting 2: 1, 10, 20, 30, 45, 60, 80, 100, 125, 150 → 1, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 55, 70, 85, 100|
|Aug 19, 2026|Changed Hunting Bestiaries to grant additional Hunting XP ([[Hunting]]) instead of additional [[Coins]].|
|Aug 24, 2026|Fixed several Critter Bestiaries awarding [[Magic Find]] instead of [[Hunting Fortune]].|
|Aug 24, 2026|Fixed several [[Lotus Atoll]] Critters using incorrect Bestiary brackets.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|The [[Shiny Pig]] Bestiary entry now displays Year of the Pig loot.|

# Bosses
Aliases: Boss
Categories: Article stubs, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Mobs, Bosses
A Boss in Hypixel SkyBlock is a mob with increased [[Health]] and has a special drop ceremony, thus creatures such as [[Slayer|Slayer Bosses]] or [[Broodmother|Tarantula Broodmothers]] are not classified as bosses, these instead should be listed as mini-bosses. The mobs currently that should be classified as bosses are the [[Magma Cube Boss]], [[Arachne]], the [[Ender Dragon]], the [[Headless Horseman]] and [[Dante]], as they are bosses that have a system based on how much damage you deal, who summoned it which determines how many drops you get.
Bosses also in order to be classified as such must be a public fight that every player can at least gain something from.
## Mechanics
Some enemies ( usually BOSS mobs ) have a [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]] which reduces the damage they take above a defined [[Damage Calculation|damage cap threshold]]. If damage exceeds this threshold, the excess is multiplied by the excess multiplier which has a value 0.1 in most cases:
  Damagepost-damageCap=0.1×(Damagepre-damageCap−damageCap)+damageCap If damage does not exceed the [[Damage Calculation|threshold]], damage will not be adjusted.
Mobs can have more than one [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]]. In such cases, the [[Damage Calculation|damage caps]] are applied in order of lowest [[Damage Calculation|threshold]] first, then ascending.

# Catacombs Rooms/Trinity
Categories: Article stubs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.

[Trinity]
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Size: 1x1
Structure: Square
Secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]]): 4
Crypts ([[Crypt Undead]]): 0
Trinity is a Rare [[Catacombs Rooms|Room]] that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Structure
- Trinity is square-shaped, containing 3 levels somewhat spiraling upwards in sharp turns, leading to [[Trinity]].
## Trivia
- [[Trinity]] can be added as an [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]], requiring 2 [[Revive Stone|Revive Stones]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Released The Catacombs Dungeon.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]).|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Trinity as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
Linked from: [[Catacombs Rooms]], [[Catacombs Rooms/Catacombs Rooms Table]], [[Dungeon Secrets]], [[Trinity]]

# Chocolate Eggs
Aliases: Chocolate Breakfast Egg, Chocolate Brunch Egg, Chocolate Dinner Egg, Chocolate Déjeuner Egg, Chocolate Egg, Chocolate Lunch Egg, Chocolate Rabbit Egg, Chocolate Rabbit Eggs, Chocolate Supper Egg
Categories: Article stubs, Confirmations needed
[Chocolate Egg]
During [[Hoppity's Hunt]], Chocolate Eggs will randomly spawn around the world. They are announced in chat as they appear.
## Mechanics
Players can search manually or use an [[Egglocator]] to locate them. Once found, they can be clicked to claim a random [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]].
A total of 6 eggs can be active at once, two of each time of day. When a new egg appears, the oldest existing one will disappear. Egg locations are consistent between servers and spawn at 7 AM, 2 PM, and 9 PM [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]].
When the player finds a chocolate egg, one of the following chat messages will be displayed with the location depending on the type of chocolate egg:
|Chocolate Egg|In-game Spawn Time|Real-world Spawn Time|Message|
|-|-|-|-|
|Breakfast Egg|7 AM|XX:00:50 XX:20:50 XX:40:50|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Breakfast Egg [Location message]!|
|Brunch Egg|7 AM|XX:00:50 XX:20:50 XX:40:50|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Brunch Egg [Location message]!|
|Lunch Egg|2 PM|XX:06:40 XX:26:40 XX:46:40|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Lunch Egg [Location message]!|
|Déjeuner Egg|2 PM|XX:06:40 XX:26:40 XX:46:40|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Déjeuner Egg [Location message]!|
|Dinner Egg|9 PM|XX:12:30 XX:32:30 XX:52:30|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Dinner Egg [Location message]!|
|Supper Egg|9 PM|XX:12:30 XX:32:30 XX:52:30|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Chocolate Supper Egg [Location message]!|
|Hitman Egg|Rabbit Hitman|Rabbit Hitman|HOPPITY'S HUNT You found a Hitman Egg!|
### Hitman Egg
If any Chocolate Egg has been missed or skipped, a Rabbit Hitman will auto collect it to be opened later on as long as there is an available slot. After opening a Hitman Egg, 20 minutes are added to the cooldown until it becomes available again. Hitman Eggs are able to collect unique location progress and [[Chocolate Rabbits|Resident Rabbits]] from other Islands. The cooldown can be redused by up to 25% from the [[Attributes|Hitman Cooldown]] attribute.
## Chocolate Rabbits
### Rabbit Chance
Rarity of an obtained [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] depends on the weighted table:
|Rabbit Rarity|Factory Tier; [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] (same as above)|Base Weight|Base Chance|All Buffs Weight|All Buffs Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|608|60.38% (1 in 1.66)|608|51.38% (1 in 1.95)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|250|24.83% (1 in 4.03)|350|29.58% (1 in 3.38)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|2|100|9.93% (1 in 10.07)|150|12.68% (1 in 7.89)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|3|30|2.98% (1 in 33.57)|45|3.80% (1 in 26.3)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|4|10|0.99% (1 in 100.7)|16|1.35% (1 in 73.96)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|5|4|0.40% (1 in 251.75)|6.4|0.54% (1 in 184.91)|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|6|4|0.40% (1 in 251.75)|6.4|0.54% (1 in 184.91)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|6|1|0.099% (1 in 1,007)|1.6|0.14% (1 in 739.63)|
Additionally, every 250th [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] is guaranteed to be [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and every 1,250th is guaranteed to be [[Rarity|DIVINE]].
Starting at [[Chocolate Factory]] 6, [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit Rabbits]] can be obtained. They have the same weight as a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rabbit. If player obtained every [[Rarity|DIVINE]] [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]], [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rabbit [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]] is replaced with a random [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit]] rabbit. If every Chocobit is already taken, it's replaced with a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rabbit. Progress can be viewed using [[Pity and RNG Meters|/pity]] command.
All rabbits of the same rarity have equal (100) weight. Starting at [[Chocolate Factory]] 5, [[Chocolate Rabbits|Faction Rabbits]] can be obtained. They have half the weight (50) of a normal rabbit. Base weight can be increased with certain methods (additive):
|Source|Weight Increase|Rabbits Affected|
|-|-|-|
|[[Prestige Chocolate Realm]] accessory line|2-10%|Unobtained Rabbits|
|Rabbit Hotspot ([[Chocolate Rabbits]])|50%|Only if on certain island|
Chance of getting a higher rarity [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] can be increased with certain methods (additive):
|Source|Weight Increase|Rabbit Rarity|
|-|-|-|
|Rabbit Shrine|2-40%|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])+|
|Napoleon Rabbit|10%|RARE ([[Rarity]])+|
|Legendary Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|Mythic Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
|Divine Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
### Duplicate Rabbits
Having a full Rabbit Barn or getting duplicate Rabbits in the [[Chocolate Factory]] will give the player [[Chocolate]] based on the rarity of the Rabbit and the player's current Chocolate per Second.
|Rarity|Base Amount; (seconds of production)|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|150 (2m 30s)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|300 (5m)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|750 (12m 30s)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1,500 (25m)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|4,000 (1h 6m 40s)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|8,000 (2h 13m 20s)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|15,000 (4h 10m)|
In addition, each duplicate rabbit found gives +10% of the base amount, up to +100% at the 10th duplicate.
Amount of [[Chocolate]] gained can also be increased with certain methods (additive):
|Source|Chocolate Boost|
|-|-|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Rabbit Pet]]|33%|
|King Rabbit|30%|
The formula is the following, where Duplicates Found is inclusive of the rabbit that you just found. Therefore, your first duplicate will already grant a 10% bonus on top of the base amount.
Chocolate Gained=Chocolate per Second×Base Amount×(1+min⁡(Duplicates Found,10)×0.1)×OtherMultipliers
### Stray Rabbits
Stray Rabbit is guaranteed to appear in 30 seconds after collecting an egg. It has a 10% (or 20% with Omega [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Rabbit) to be replaced with a [[Chocolate Factory|Golden Rabbit]].

# Chocolate Rabbits
Aliases: Abi, Alley, Anchor, Aster, Avalanche, Chasm, Chocobit, Chocobit Rabbit, Chocobit Rabbits, Chocobit Tower, Chocobits, Chocolate Rabbit, Clover, Cobble, Coconut, Daisy, Dune, Faction Rabbit, Faction Rabbits, Glacier, Graffiti, Haybale, Hazel, Horizon, Icefur, Iris, Juniper, Lagoon, Lavender, Mistral, Mocktail, Moss, Nectar, Neon, Palm, Peon, Rabbit Factions, Rabbit Hotspot, Resident Rabbit, Root, Rosebud, Sandy, Scree, Seabun, Seashell, Shortcut, Silo, Skyrise, Speedy, Starfish, Subway, Summit, Surf, Tan, Taxi, Traffic, Tundra, Tunnel, Twigy, Valley
Categories: Article stubs, Bad currency number
[Chocolate Rabbits]
Chocolate Rabbits are collectibles found from interacting with [[Chocolate Eggs]] during [[Hoppity's Hunt]].
## Obtaining
Chocolate Rabbits can be obtained from different sources, the maximum rarity of Chocolate Rabbits that can be obtained depends on the level of the [[Chocolate Factory]]. Some rabbits can only be found after certain requirements have been met.
### Hoppity's Hunt
During [[Hoppity's Hunt]], [[Chocolate Eggs]] can be found all across [[SkyBlock]]. Interacting with the eggs will result in a random Chocolate Rabbit to be found, if said rabbit has already been found or the [[Chocolate Factory|Rabbit Barn]] is full, [[Chocolate]] will be rewarded instead based on the rarity of the found rabbit.
### Hoppity
Every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] during [[Hoppity's Hunt]], [[Hoppity]] shows up and sells various Chocolate Rabbits, which varies in rarity from any rabbit up to [[Rarity|EPIC]]. Buying a rabbit while already owning it is treated the same as getting a duplicate and awards [[Chocolate]] instead.
### Stray Rabbits
[[Chocolate Factory|Stray Rabbits]] have a chance to drop a [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Egg]], or a unique Chocolate Rabbit, like El Dorado or Fish the Rabbit.
### Chocolate Factory Milestones
Reaching certain all-time [[Chocolate]] milestones and spending certain amounts of [[Chocolate]] in the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] will give the player unique Chocolate Rabbits that cannot be obtained anywhere else.
Rabbits from milestones cannot be claimed if the Rabbit Barn is full.
## Usage
Chocolate Rabbits are used to boost [[Chocolate]] production. The more unique rabbits found, the more chocolate the factory produces. The rarity of a rabbit affects the boost it gives.
|Rarity|Bonus [[Chocolate]]|Bonus Multiplier|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+1|+0.002x|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+2|+0.003x|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+4|+0.004x|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+10|+0.005x|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||+0.02x|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Varied|Varied|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])||+0.025x|
[[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Chocolate Rabbits give different perks instead of the usual [[Chocolate]] boost.
|Name|Effect|
|-|-|
|Dante|+50 [[Chocolate]] per second|
|Einstein|Decreases the time it takes to charge Time Tower ([[Chocolate Factory]]) to 7 hours|
|Galaxy|+0.05x [[Chocolate]] per second|
|King|All duplicate rabbits give 30% more [[Chocolate]]|
|Mu|Increases Time Tower ([[Chocolate Factory]]) production by +0.7x|
|Napoleon|Increases the odds of finding RARE ([[Rarity]]) and higher Chocolate Rabbits by 10%|
|Omega|Doubles the odds of finding Golden Rabbits|
|Sigma|Each of your Mythic Rabbits grants +5 [[Chocolate]] per second.|
|Zest Zephyr|+30 and +0.03x [[Chocolate]] per second|
|Zeta|Reduces the cost of your Employee upgrades by 1%|
|Zorro|Unlocks [[Zorro's Cape]] in the Chocolate Shop ([[Chocolate Factory]])|
Each [[Rarity|DIVINE]] Chocolate Rabbit increases the max [[Chocolate Factory|Employee]] level by 1.
### Faction Rabbits
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Rabbit Faction system is unlocked at Chocolate Factory 5. After choosing a faction, the player will be able to drop Faction Rabbits from their own faction. Faction Rabbits will not immediately be added to Hoppity's Collection: instead, they will drop as an item where they can be traded on the [[Bazaar]] or added to Hoppity's Collection.
After joining a faction, the player can only find [[Rarity|COMMON]] Faction Rabbits. To find higher rarity Faction Rabbits, the player must spend [[Chocolate]] to upgrade their faction tier.
|Level|Max Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|1|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]]|
|2|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|2,500,000,000 [[Chocolate]]|
|3|RARE ([[Rarity]])|5,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]]|
|4|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|7,500,000,000 [[Chocolate]]|
|5|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]]|
It is possible to switch the Rabbit Faction for the cost of 1,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] at any point. Upon joining a new/the same Rabbit Faction, the player needs to buy the faction tiers again.
### Chocobit Rabbits
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Chocobits are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Chocolate Rabbits. Each Chocobit the player owns provides a +0.2x Bonus Multiplier. There are 33,333 unique Chocobits, each labeled with a number from #1 to #33,333. A Chocobit can only be owned by one player. Chocobits live in the Chocobit Tower, so they do not take up space in the Rabbit Barn.
Chocobits expire at the end of the [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] by default, but the player can pay 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to extend their expiration by 1 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]]. Once expired, they give 2x the [[Chocolate]] they would have otherwise produced over that [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]], which equals BaseChocolatePerSecond×0.2×(60×60×124×2).
For each Chocobits that expires at the end of the [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]], the player will recieve the chat message:
HOPPITY'S HUNT: You lost Chocobits#_____! They are now FREE! Until someone else finds them...

### Rabbit Hotspots and Resident Rabbits
During [[Hoppity's Hunt]], random Chocolate Rabbits will be assigned to a random island, which makes that rabbit have a 50% more chance to be obtained from a [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Egg]] there.
Some Chocolate Rabbits are Residents of a certain island, which means that they can only appear in that island.
## Trivia
- Fish the Rabbit is the only Rabbit to have its rarity change.
- Stray-only chocolate rabbits (Fish the Rabbit and El Dorado) provide as much chocolate as a duplicate.
  - Additionally, they increase the duplicate counter when clicked.
  - El Dorado requires to be clicked 3 times to actually obtain, and every duplicate requires another 3 clicks.
- [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] players have dungeon requirements automatically unlocked since they cannot play dungeons, therefore Necron and Kaeman are automatically unlocked for them.
- If a player has more than 21 Chocobits a new page will open containing 21 more slots.
## History

|Aug 25, 2026|Added Faction Rabbits and Chocobits.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Doubled the drop rates of MYTHIC and DIVINE Rabbits, and reduced their pity accordingly.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Chocolate Rabbits.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Added DIVINE ([[Rarity]]) Rabbits.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Added +16 COMMON ([[Rarity]]) +17 UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) +6 RARE ([[Rarity]]) +7 LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) +4 + 5 DIVINE ([[Rarity]]).|
|Jul 9, 2024|0.20.4|Increased Fish the Rabbit rarity from UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) to RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Added 5 [[Backwater Bayou]] resident rabbits.|
|May 26, 2026|0.25|Added 5 [[Lotus Atoll]] resident rabbits.|

# Collection Display
Categories: Article stubs, Uncommon items, Items, Events, Bingo
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Collection Display]
Type: Cosmetic
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 30 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COLLECTION_DISPLAY
Tooltip: Show off your collection progress!; UNCOMMON COSMETIC
The Collection Display set to its default value, Cocoa Beans.
Collection Display is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Cosmetic. It displays the player's progress in a single [[Collections|Collection]].
## Obtaining
It can bought from the [[Bingo|Bingo Shop]] for 30 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]].
## Usage
The Collection Display allows for display of any single collection in the [[Farming]], [[Mining]], [[Combat]], [[Foraging]], or [[Fishing]] collections. It can not display Boss Collections. In [[Co-op]] profiles, this item can function as a leaderboard between all players on the profile.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 31, 2024|Collection Display is no longer soulbound.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 29, 2021|Added Collection Display.|
|Dec 1, 2021|Collection Display becomes obtainable.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Collection Display is no longer soulbound.|
Linked from: [[Bingo]], [[Bingo (NPC)]], [[Display (disambiguation)]], [[Museum]], [[Museum/Items]], [[Museum/Items/Special]]

|Splash God Splash|When a player splashes high level potions in a group, mainly in empty lobbies. These players often charge a fee for players wanting to take the effects of the potions. This doesn't occur much outside of the Bingo event.||
|Collat/Collateral|An item or money temporarily given as assurance in trades, services or loans to signify being legitimate. For example, if somebody is putting upgrades on your item that you cannot for whatever reason, they may give you items or money so that if they were to steal your item, you would still have their collateral as backup. As well as preventing scams, collateral can also be used to scam if given in inaccurate amounts.||

|___ Midas|[[Midas' Sword]] or [[Midas Staff]], often with the coins used to purchase the sword in the [[Dark Auction]] (See ability for explanation)||

|NW/Net|Networth, total value of player's items. NW can also mean [[Nether Wart]]|Not Hypixel-specific|
|MMM|Money Making Method||
|Overpay|A term used when a player on the Dark Auction bids more money than the supposed worth of an item. This causes the player to be unable to profit from the item as it'll sell for less than the player paid for.||
|Dupe|A term for a glitch or exploit that allows for duplication of items or coins. When used in the context of dungeons, this means two people playing as the same class. It can also mean getting a rare drop that you already have.|Not SkyBlock-specific|
|Techno/Technoblade/The Blade|A famous YouTuber that had participated in the "Potato War" between him and im_a_squid_kid. He was also the [[Resistance Leader]].||
|Flux/owoflux|[[Overflux Power Orb]], though "flux" alone could refer to almost any power orb.||

|_ bil/b|[[Coins]], in billions|Not Hypixel-specific|
|HotM|[[Heart of the Mountain]]||
|Boss Trading|Slayer Trading. The act of letting other people with slayer quests kill your boss so that when your boss is killed, their boss immediately spawns soon after. This is usually done to speed up slayer grinding.||
|Lowballing|The act of buying someone's item for a lesser amount than the item's supposed worth.||
|Exotics|An exotic is a leather armor that is dyed differently than it's supposed color. Due to the difficulty of acquiring a differently dyed armor, exotics are worth hundreds of millions of coins, sometimes even billions.||
|RAT or Rat|RAT is an acronym that stands for Remote Access Trojan. It is a term used for illegally modified mods with token loggers. If one installed these "rat" mods, they risk their account being stolen. When one's account is hacked, it's often called being "ratted".||

|Gdrag|[[Golden Dragon Pet]]||
|GR/God Roll|A good pair of [[Attributes]] on weapons, armor or equipment from the Crimson Isle. an example is Mana Regeneration and Mana Pool (MPMR) combo on an Aurora Armor piece.||
|Wipe|a term used when a SkyBlock player has been deleted of all progress including items, skills, backpack and ender chests etc. Wipes often happen when Hypixel detects boosting on a profile or other punishable offense.||
|Cords/Coords|Coordinates||

## Dungeon-related Abbreviations
|Abbreviation|Actual Meaning|
|-|-|
|R|Ready. Used in dungeon parties to let the leader know you're ready to start.|
|NR|Not Ready. Used in dungeon parties to let the leader and other party members you're not ready to start.|
|E, F1-7 and M1-7|Entrance, Floor 1-7 and Master Mode Floor 1-7|
|Dupe|Two people playing as the same class. When used outside of dungeons, dupe means a glitch or exploit that allows for duplication of items or coins|
|LA|[[Lost Adventurer]]|
|AA|[[Angry Archaeologist]]|
|SA|[[Shadow Assassin]] or [[Shadow Assassin Armor]]|
|FA|[[Frozen Adventurer]]|
|ZK|[[Zombie Knight]] or [[Zombie Knight Armor]]|
|ZS|[[Zombie Soldier]] or [[Zombie Soldier Armor]]|
|Hyp/Hype|[[Hyperion]]|
|Blaze|Refers to the "Higher or Lower" puzzle where one has to shoot blazes from either lowest to highest HP(if the chest is at the bottom) or highest to lowest HP(if the chest is in the top).|
|TTT|Refers to the Tic Tac Toe puzzle.|
|BD/Defuse|Refers to the (removed) Bomb Defuse ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms]]) puzzle on Floor 7.|
|Valk|[[Valkyrie]]|
|OFA|[[One For All]] ultimate enchantment|
|SF|[[Shadow Fury]]|
|Frag run|A money making method involving running Floor 7 solo and making your way to the Blood Room as fast as possible, you kill the Sadan Giant that the Watcher spawns, grab the drop ("fragment"), and restart the run.|
|BR|Blood Rush, strategy in which one player (usually done by the Mage or both Mage and Archer in Master Mode) is tasked to rush through all the rooms with Wither Keys and get to the Blood Room as fast as possible.|
|BC|Blood Camp, strategy in which one player (usually done by the Mage) is tasked to kill mobs in Blood Room for them to spawn faster.|
|PF|Party Finder|
|LC/RC (In F7)|Indicates the power crystal the player is going for in [[Maxor]] Phase of Floor 7.|
|GP/YP/PP (In F7)|Green Pad, Yellow Pad, Purple Pad, referring to the 3 pads in the [[Storm]] fight. The strategy usually goes GP -> YP in normal parties or M7. Or either, the slightly faster PP -> YP in sweaty F7 parties.|
|S1-4 (In F7)|Set 1-4, referring to the 4 sets of terminals in the [[Goldor]] fight.|
|1-5 (In F7)|Refers to the different numbers assigned to the terminals in the [[Goldor]] fight, eg. if you call 2, this means you will do the 2nd terminal in all 4 sets of terminals of the bossfight. In higher level parties it's usually assumed that the Tank does 1, Mage 2, Bers 3, Arch 4, Healer Dev. The main strategy is usually a bit trickier than that but this is the base assumption.|
|Dev (In F7)|Short for Device, refers to the Devices in the [[Goldor]] Fight.|
|SS (In F7)|Simon Says, the button pressing device in the first room of the [[Goldor]] fight.|
|PD (In F7)|Pre-dev, a strategy used in Floor 7 (usually done by the Healer) to prepare the 2nd and 3rd device of the [[Goldor]] fight to be one click off being completed, while the rest of the team does the regular bossfight.|
|I4 / PRE-4 (In F7)|Instant 4th Device or Pre-4th Device, strategy used in Floor 7 (usually done by the Berserker) where you complete the 4th device with invincibility items like the [[Bonzo's Mask]], [[Spirit Mask]] and [[Phoenix Pet]] as the [[Goldor]] phase starts.|
|EE2/EE3|Early Enter 2 or 3, referring to the strategy (usually done by the Archer for EE2 and Healer for EE3) where someone uses invincibility items like the [[Bonzo's Mask]], [[Spirit Mask]] and [[Phoenix Pet]] to go into the next set of terminals before the team finishes the current set so the team can immediately leap to the middle of the next set to make it faster.|
|Core / Slingshot|A strategy (usually done by the Mage) where after completing the 2nd terminal in Set 2 they stonk their way into the Core of the [[Goldor]] fight and into Set 4 early. The name "Slingshot" comes from the idea that the person "slingshots" right through the bossfight skipping S3. The team then leaps when they are done just like early enter (but without having to wait for S3 to be done).|

A Cretan Bull is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Cretan Bull can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv47]|Blessed Cretan Bull|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|22.22% (1 in 4.5 mobs)|25,000|100|70|51|15|
|[Lv95]|Stalwart Cretan Bull|RARE ([[Rarity]])|16.67% (1 in 6 mobs)|150,000|400|142|104|31|
|[Lv135]|Venerable Cretan Bull|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|14.29% (1 in 7 mobs)|500,000|800|202|148|45|
|[Lv186]|Exalted Cretan Bull|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|13.91% (1 in 7.19 mobs)|1,500,000|2,000|279|204|62|
|[Lv216]|Empyrean Cretan Bull|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|13.84% (1 in 7.22 mobs)|3,000,000|2,750|324|237|72|
## Behavior
A Cretan Bull rams towards the player at high speeds, dealing 1k/2k/3k/4k/6k/10k [[Damage]] (based on the tier) and [[Knockback|True Knockback]] upon hitting them. After a successful hit, the Cretan Bull stops moving for 5 seconds before ramming again, although it will still inflict damage on contact.

# Crux Mobs
Categories: Article stubs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Crux Mobs refers to a group of [[Mob|Mobs]] consisting of [[Shy|Shies]], [[Shadow|Shadows]], [[Volt|Volts]], [[Scribe|Scribes]], [[Frozille|Frozilles]], [[Splatter|Splatters]], and [[Puff|Puffs]].
Their main common traits are the following :
- They drop [[Crux (disambiguation)|Cruxes]].
- Killing them contributes to upgrading the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades, granting more [[Rift Time]] and [[Crux Fortune]].
- Other than [[Puff|Puffs]], a task is required after a few hits.
Thus, they can be interpreted as a Mob Family despite the [[Rift Dimension|Rift Dimension’s]] lack of a [[Bestiary]].
Linked from: [[Crux (disambiguation)]], [[Crux Fortune]]

# Damage Calculation/Damage Cap
Categories: Article stubs
Some enemies ( usually [[Bosses|BOSS]] mobs ) have a [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]] which reduces the damage they take above a defined [[Damage Calculation|damage cap threshold]]. If damage exceeds this threshold, the excess is multiplied by the excess multiplier which has a value 0.1 in most cases:
  Damagepost-damageCap=0.1×(Damagepre-damageCap−damageCap)+damageCap If damage does not exceed the [[Damage Calculation|threshold]], damage will not be adjusted.
Mobs can have more than one [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]]. In such cases, the [[Damage Calculation|damage caps]] are applied in order of lowest [[Damage Calculation|threshold]] first, then ascending.

|[[Bladesoul]]|50,000,000 [[Health]]|1. 800,000 [[Health]]; 2. 1,200,000 [[Health]]|Multiple damage caps ([[Damage Calculation]])|

# Damage Values
Categories: Article stubs, Glitches, Mechanics
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Damage Values are used in [[SkyBlock]] to assign the correct texture to an item. [[Vanilla Objects|Vanilla items]] that become damaged can easily be turned into [[Null|nulls]], just by dropping them on the ground, and can also be given illegal textures. Damage values in vanilla are there to show how much [[Damage]] an item has taken, but SkyBlock converts the value into data values, which used to assign textures in vanilla minecraft. For example, Stone:0 is [[Stone]], where Stone:1 is [[Granite]]. A [[Vanilla Objects|vanilla]] piece of [[Stone]] without any damage taken off gets turned into SkyBlock stone, and is assigned a [[Rarity]] and other tags. The only known possible items this transformation is possible with are items fished up before the fishing update, [[Null]] [[Boots]], [[Null]] [[Fishing Rods]], and [[Null]] [[Bows]]. These could be [[Fishing|fished up]] with [[Damage]] taken away, which then can change their texture to their damage value, creating an untextured, "not full durability" item.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Damage Values.|

# Death
Categories: Article stubs, Mechanics
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Undergoing a rewrite.
See more stubs.
[Death]
Death is a game mechanic that occurs when the player's [[Health]] reaches 0 .
## Overview
Death occurs when the player's [[Health]] reaches 0 . Upon dying, the [[Player]] is returned to the island's spawn.
The player usually loses half of the [[Coins]] in their [[Purse]]. However, due to the nature of SkyBlock, the player does not lose items on death.
## Factors in Coin Loss
- Having a [[Piggy Bank]] in the [[Accessory Bag]], or having a [[Booster Cookie]] active, nullifies the loss of [[Coins]].
  - A [[Piggy Bank|Cracked Piggy Bank]] only saves 75% of the player's held coins, and a [[Piggy Bank|Broken Piggy Bank]] does not prevent loss of [[Coins]].
- [[Bank (Enchantment)|Bank]] saves 10%-50% of the player's coins on death. Wearing two pieces with [[Bank (Enchantment)|Bank V]] saves all of the player's coins.
- If the player has a [[Phoenix Pet]] active, they do not lose coins on death.
- Using an [[Emerald Blade]], or using one within a [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]], applies the Curse of Greed, nullifying the effects of anything affecting loss of [[Coins]] on death.
- While in the [[Catacombs]], dying does not cause coin loss, instead, the player is turned into a ghost and can be revived.
## Preventing Death
- If the player is wearing a [[Spirit Mask]] and would take fatal damage, they are protected for that damage and all other damage for 3 seconds. Additionally, they gain +50 [[Speed]] for the same amount of time.
- If the player is in the [[Catacombs]], is wearing a [[Bonzo's Mask]], and would take fatal damage, they are protected for that damage and all other damage for 3 seconds. Additionally, they gain +20 [[Strength]] for the same amount of time and regenerate their entire health.
- If the player has a [[Saving Grace]] in their inventory and would take fatal damage, they are instead teleported to their [[Private Island]]. However, it is common for this to fail.
- If the player is on [[The End]], has a [[Remnant of the Eye]], and would take fatal damage, they instead gain invincibility for a short period of time and regenerate to full health.
- If the player has a [[Phoenix Pet]] active and would take fatal damage, they instead gain from +10.1-30 [[Strength]] and gain invincibility for 2-4 seconds, with a 1 minute cooldown.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Death.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Potion effects are no longer removed on death.|
Linked from: [[Bank]], [[Banker]], [[Banker Barry]], [[Banker Bonsai]], [[Banker Broadjaw]], [[Belanor]], [[Bruto]], [[Player]], [[Saving Grace]]

# Desert Whispers
Categories: Article stubs, More info needed, Currencies
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Desert Whispers]
Type: [[Currency]]
Desert Whispers are a type of [[Currency]] that can be used to upgrade [[Heart of the Forest]] perks from [[Heart of the Forest|HotF 4]] to [[Heart of the Forest|HotF 8]].
## Obtaining
Desert Whispers can be obtained from various sources.
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) per log cut on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]|
|[[Tree Gifts]]|[More Info Needed] per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]]) on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]|
|[[Floor Drops]]|250 Desert Whispers|
|[[Ruby Veilshroom]]|[More Info Needed] per [[Ruby Veilshroom]] harvested|
|Miria's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|[More Info Needed] for reaching certain brackets|
The amount of Desert Whispers gained can be increased in various ways.
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Center of the Forest (Level 3) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|2 Desert Whispers per log cut; 20 Desert Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|Daily Wishes Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|400–40,000 Desert Whispers for the first [[Helix Log]] you cut on the [[Torrhus Canyon]] every day|
|Monster Hunters Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|20 Desert Whispers when [[Hunting]] a monster|
|Tree Whisperer Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|200 Desert Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|250 Gifts Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|20 Desert Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]]) for the first 250 Tree Gifts in a day|
|Miria's Milestones (II-V) ([[Miria]])|+50%-200% Desert Whispers from Miria's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|
## Usage
Desert Whispers are used to upgrade [[Heart of the Forest]] perks between Heart of the Forest 4-8.
## Trivia
- The maximum amount of Desert Whispers a player may have is 2 billion.
- Players with over 12,500,000 [[Forest Whispers]] before the release of the [[Torrhus Canyon]] had excess [[Forest Whispers]] converted to Desert Whispers at a 1:1 ratio.

Dragontail is a location on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dragontail, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dragontail
■ Meet the Piglins, members of the Barbarian Faction.
■ Talk to Chief Scorn.
■ Earn Reputation through the daily Town Board missions.
## Purpose
Dragontail has many locations inside of it:
- [[Dragontail Auction House]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Auction House]])
- [[Dragontail Bank]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Bank]])
- [[Dragontail Bazaar]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Bazaar]])
- [[Dragontail Blacksmith]] (Works like the [[Dungeon Hub|Dungeon]] [[Blacksmith]])
- [[Chief's Hut]] (Shows daily tasks and is where [[Chief Scorn]] is)
- [[Minion Shop]] (Shop with nether-related Tier 12 [[Minions]])
- [[Dragontail Townsquare]] (Heart of Dragontail)
- [[The Bastion]] (Skull structure north of Dragontail, guarded by [[Barbarian Guard|Barbarian Guards]])
- The Brewery (Building in the north of Dragontail, with [[Grog]] and a secret number-pad)

The Dungeon Journal is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Admin-only]] item that was spotted in 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] TorWolf's inventory in July of 2021. The item appears to hold [[Journal|Journal Entries]].
